Kết quả chấm bài

Substantiated behaviors: sample_dl.exe execution from cmd.exe (E02), DNS resolution to update.labnet.local (E03), HTTP GET /files/upd.bin (E03), scheduled task creation (E04), SHA256 hash (E01). Defects: 1. Missing registry key addition (E05), 2. Placeholder E07 in file system, 3. Missing network payload download evidence, 4. Inconsistent PID (2726 vs 9308), 5. Unresolved evidence reference E06, 6. Coarse timeline (only 4 entries vs 8 in golden), 7. Privilege misread (HKCU vs HKLM), 8. Unsupported causal claim about stage-1 downloader, 9. Missing payload drop evidence (E04), 10. Missing environmental outcome (no logoff/logon test).

Spawn schtasks.exe /create /tn "SystemHealthCheck" /tr "%APPDATA%\SysHelper\syshelper.exe" /sc onlogon /f → tạo scheduled task tự chạy khi logon.<br>4. Mẫu mã hoá file người dùng. |
